// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/presentation/components/cockpit/CockpitCentralStage.tsx =====

import React from 'react';

// --- Tipagens e Contratos ---
import { Client } from '@/core/client/types/client';
import { CRMDeal } from '../../../domain/types';
import { CrudConfig, FormMode } from '@/core/components/crud/types';

// --- Componentes Filhos e Motores Injetáveis ---
import { CrudFormOrchestrator } from '@/core/components/crud/views/form/CrudFormOrchestrator'; 
import { ClientDashboard } from './client-dashboard/ClientDashboard'; // 👑 O Retorno do Rei (Memória de IA)

// --- Mutadores (CQRS - Padrão Command) ---
import { useDealMutation } from '../../../application/hooks/useDealMutation';
import { useClientMutation } from '@/core/client/hooks/useClientMutation';

export type CockpitFormType = 'NONE' | 'CLIENT' | 'DEAL';

export interface CockpitCentralStageProps {
    client: Client | null; 
    activeForm: CockpitFormType;
    dealToEdit?: CRMDeal | null;
    
    dealConfig?: CrudConfig<CRMDeal, any>;
    clientConfig?: CrudConfig<Client, any>; 
    
    onCloseForm: () => void;
    onEditClientRequest: () => void;
    onUpdateSuccess: () => void;
}

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * 🎭 PILAR 2: COCKPIT CENTRAL STAGE (A Área Nobre)
 * @description Roteador visual inline. Delega a edição/criação para o Motor V4,
 * e a visualização rica (Memória de Curto/Médio Prazo) para o Dashboard de IA.
 * * 🏆 PADRÃO DE QUALIDADE CLASSE OURO:
 * - Domain-Driven Design (DDD): O Motor CRUD atua apenas como "ferramenta de entrada". 
 * A leitura estratégica e o processamento de IA continuam no coração do domínio CRM.
 * - Key Forcing (Reset Absoluto): Força o React a destruir e recriar o DOM
 * ao trocar de entidade, eliminando qualquer risco de vazamento de estado (Memory Leaks).
 * ============================================================================
 */
export const CockpitCentralStage: React.FC<CockpitCentralStageProps> = ({
    client,
    activeForm,
    dealToEdit,
    dealConfig,
    clientConfig,
    onCloseForm,
    onEditClientRequest,
    onUpdateSuccess
}) => {
    
    const { createDeal, updateDeal } = useDealMutation();
    const { createClient, updateClient } = useClientMutation();

    // ========================================================================
    // 📝 RENDER STATE 1: MODO CRIAÇÃO / EDIÇÃO DE CLIENTE (O Motor V4 assume)
    // ========================================================================
    if (activeForm === 'CLIENT') {
        
        if (!clientConfig) {
            return (
                <div className="flex-1 w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-900 rounded-2xl border border-red-900/50">
                    <p className="text-red-400 font-bold">Erro: Configuração do Cliente (clientConfig) não injetada pelo Maestro.</p>
                </div>
            );
        }

        return (
            <div className="flex-1 w-full h-full bg-gray-900/80 rounded-2xl border border-gray-700/80 flex flex-col overflow-hidden animate-fade-in-up shadow-2xl relative">
                <CrudFormOrchestrator<Client, any>
                    // 💡 KEY FORCING: Nasce um formulário 100% virgem a cada clique
                    key={`client-form-${client?.id || 'new'}`}
                    isOpen={true}
                    onClose={onCloseForm}
                    config={clientConfig}
                    initialData={client || undefined} 
                    presentationMode="inline" 
                    formMode={FormMode.EDIT}
                    onSubmit={async (data) => {
                        try {
                            if (client) {
                                await updateClient(client.id, data);
                            } else {
                                await createClient(data); 
                            }
                            onUpdateSuccess();
                            onCloseForm();
                        } catch (err) {
                            console.error("Falha ao salvar Cliente:", err);
                            throw err; 
                        }
                    }}
                />
            </div>
        );
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // 💼 RENDER STATE 2: MODO CRIAÇÃO / EDIÇÃO DE DEAL
    // ========================================================================
    if (activeForm === 'DEAL') {
        
        if (!client) {
            return (
                <div className="flex-1 w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-gray-900 rounded-2xl border border-red-900/50 p-6 text-center">
                    <p className="text-red-400 font-bold mb-2">Erro de Consistência</p>
                    <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">É necessário selecionar ou salvar um cliente antes de gerenciar oportunidades.</p>
                </div>
            );
        }

        if (!dealConfig) {
            return (
                <div className="flex-1 w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-900 rounded-2xl border border-red-900/50">
                    <p className="text-red-400 font-bold">Erro: Configuração da Oportunidade (dealConfig) não injetada.</p>
                </div>
            );
        }

        return (
            <div className="flex-1 w-full h-full bg-gray-900/80 rounded-2xl border border-gray-700/80 flex flex-col overflow-hidden animate-fade-in-up shadow-2xl relative">
                <CrudFormOrchestrator<CRMDeal, any>
                    // 💡 KEY FORCING ACOPLADO: Se mudar a deal ou o cliente, o form limpa
                    key={`deal-form-${dealToEdit?.id || 'new'}-${client.id}`}
                    isOpen={true}
                    onClose={onCloseForm}
                    config={dealConfig}
                    initialData={dealToEdit || undefined}
                    presentationMode="inline" 
                    formMode={FormMode.EDIT}
                    onSubmit={async (data) => {
                        try {
                            if (dealToEdit) {
                                await updateDeal(dealToEdit.id, data);
                            } else {
                                await createDeal({ ...data, clientId: client.id });
                            }
                            onUpdateSuccess();
                            onCloseForm();
                        } catch (err) {
                            console.error("Falha na transação da Oportunidade:", err);
                            throw err;
                        }
                    }}
                />
            </div>
        );
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // 👁️ RENDER STATE 3: MODO LEITURA (Dashboard de IA & Timeline)
    // @description Se o activeForm for 'NONE' e houver um cliente, exibimos
    // o Cérebro de Relacionamento Master.
    // ========================================================================
    if (activeForm === 'NONE' && client) {
        return (
            <div className="flex-1 w-full h-full animate-fade-in">
                <ClientDashboard 
                    // 💡 KEY FORCING MÁXIMO: Ao clicar num novo cliente no menu lateral,
                    // a IA zera todo o estado interno, fecha websockets e refaz as queries.
                    key={`ai-dashboard-${client.id}`}
                    
                    client={client}
                    mode="VIEW" 
                    onModeChange={(mode) => {
                        // Delega a ação de edição solicitada internamente no Dashboard
                        // para o Maestro Central orquestrar a troca de tela.
                        if (mode === 'EDIT') {
                            onEditClientRequest();
                        }
                    }} 
                    onUpdateSuccess={onUpdateSuccess} 
                />
            </div>
        );
    }

    // Fallback: Se não for nenhum dos 3 estados (ex: NONE sem cliente), retorna vazio
    return null;
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/presentation/components/cockpit/CockpitSidebarLeft.tsx =====

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaRegHandshake, FaBoxOpen, FaInbox } from 'react-icons/fa';

// --- Tipagens ---
import { Client } from '@/core/client/types/client';
import { CRMDeal } from '../../../domain/types';

// --- Motores Visuais (V4) ---
import { ClientRegistry } from '@/core/client/registries/ClientRegistry';
import { DealRegistry } from '../../registries/DealRegistry';

/**
 * @type LeftTab
 * @description Define as abas ativas na metade inferior da barra lateral.
 */
type LeftTab = 'DEALS' | 'ORDERS';

/**
 * @interface CockpitSidebarLeftProps
 * @description Contrato estrito de comunicação entre a Barra Esquerda e o Maestro (CockpitView).
 * * 🏆 CLASSE OURO: Inversão de Controle (IoC). A barra lateral não abre modais nem
 * controla o palco central. Ela apenas emite "Intenções" (Requests) para o componente pai.
 * E agora atua como conduíte de estado de seleção (Prop Drilling) para iluminar os cards ativos.
 */
interface CockpitSidebarLeftProps {
    /** @description O cliente atualmente focado no CRM (Palco Central) */
    selectedClient: Client | null;
    
    /** @description 💡 A oportunidade (Deal) atualmente focada no CRM (Palco Central) */
    selectedDeal: CRMDeal | null;
    
    /** @description Ação: Usuário selecionou um cliente na lista */
    onSelectClient: (client: Client | null) => void;
    
    /** @description Ação: Usuário clicou em "Novo" na lista de clientes */
    onOpenNewClient: () => void;
    
    /** @description Ação: Usuário clicou no ícone de Lápis/Engrenagem na lista de clientes */
    onEditClientRequest: (client: Client | Partial<Client>) => void;
    
    /** @description Ação: Usuário clicou em "Novo Deal" ou "Editar Deal" na lista inferior */
    onDealFormRequest: (deal?: CRMDeal | Partial<CRMDeal> | null) => void;
}

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * 🎛️ PILAR 1: COCKPIT SIDEBAR LEFT (O Centro de Comando)
 * @description Responsável por definir o "Contexto Global" do CRM. 
 * Metade superior: Lista de Clientes (Motor V4). 
 * Metade inferior: Registros vinculados ao cliente (Deals, Pedidos).
 * ============================================================================
 */
export const CockpitSidebarLeft: React.FC<CockpitSidebarLeftProps> = ({
    selectedClient,
    selectedDeal, // 💡 Recebemos a intenção de foco do Maestro
    onSelectClient,
    onOpenNewClient,
    onEditClientRequest,
    onDealFormRequest
}) => {
    // Estado local para controlar a aba inferior (Isolado, não afeta o resto do sistema)
    const [activeLeftTab, setActiveLeftTab] = useState<LeftTab>('DEALS');

    return (
        <aside className="w-[340px] flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col h-full bg-gray-900 border-r border-gray-800 z-10 shadow-[4px_0_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2)]">
            
            {/* ================================================================
                METADE SUPERIOR: Motor de Busca e Lista de Clientes (V4)
            ================================================================ */}
            <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 flex flex-col border-b border-gray-800">
                <ClientRegistry 
                    variant="compact"
                    // 💡 O FIO CONDUTOR DO CLIENTE: Informa ao Registry qual ID destacar
                    selectedItemId={selectedClient?.id} 
                    
                    // Emite a intenção de seleção para o Maestro
                    onSelectClient={onSelectClient}
                    // Transforma o request unificado do Motor V4 nas ações específicas do Maestro
                    onFormRequest={(clientToEdit) => {
                        if (clientToEdit) {
                            onEditClientRequest(clientToEdit);
                        } else {
                            onOpenNewClient();
                        }
                    }}
                />
            </div>

            {/* ================================================================
                METADE INFERIOR: Dossiê Secundário (Deals & Pedidos do Cliente)
            ================================================================ */}
            <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 flex flex-col bg-gray-900/50">
                
                {/* Cabeçalho de Abas Inferiores */}
                <div className="flex border-b border-gray-800/80 flex-shrink-0 bg-gray-800/20">
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveLeftTab('DEALS')}
                        className={`flex-1 py-3 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest transition-all border-b-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${
                            activeLeftTab === 'DEALS' 
                                ? 'border-accent-primary text-accent-primary bg-accent-primary/5' 
                                : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-800'
                        }`}
                    >
                        <FaRegHandshake size={14} /> Deals
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveLeftTab('ORDERS')}
                        className={`flex-1 py-3 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest transition-all border-b-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${
                            activeLeftTab === 'ORDERS' 
                                ? 'border-accent-primary text-accent-primary bg-accent-primary/5' 
                                : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-800'
                        }`}
                    >
                        <FaBoxOpen size={14} /> Pedidos
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* Área de Visualização do Dossiê Secundário */}
                <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 p-2 overflow-hidden relative">
                    {!selectedClient ? (
                        // ZERO STATE: Nenhum cliente selecionado no topo
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-center p-6 opacity-40">
                            <FaInbox className="text-3xl mb-3 text-gray-500" />
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium">Selecione um cliente acima para carregar as oportunidades.</p>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        // CONTEÚDO ATIVO
                        <>
                            {activeLeftTab === 'DEALS' && (
                                <DealRegistry 
                                    variant="compact" 
                                    clientId={selectedClient.id} 
                                    
                                    // 💡 O FIO CONDUTOR DO DEAL: Informa ao Registry qual ID destacar
                                    selectedItemId={selectedDeal?.id} 
                                    
                                    onFormRequest={onDealFormRequest} 
                                />
                            )}
                            {activeLeftTab === 'ORDERS' && (
                                <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-full border-2 border-dashed border-gray-800 rounded-xl m-2 text-gray-600 text-xs">
                                    Módulo de Pedidos em breve.
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </aside>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/presentation/components/cockpit/CockpitSidebarRight.tsx =====

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaWhatsapp, FaTelegram, FaPaperPlane, FaCalendarAlt, FaTimes } from 'react-icons/fa';

// --- Tipagens e Contratos ---
import { Client } from '@/core/client/types/client';

// --- Motores Injetáveis (Widgets de Omnichannel) ---
import { WhatsAppWidget } from '../shared/WhatsAppWidget';
import { SchedulerWidget } from '@/modules/scheduler/components/SchedulerWidget';

/**
 * @type OmnichannelTab
 * @description Define os módulos disponíveis no painel de comunicação.
 * null = Painel recolhido (colapsado).
 */
type OmnichannelTab = 'WA' | 'TG' | 'AGENDA' | null;

/**
 * @interface CockpitSidebarRightProps
 * @description Contrato estrito do Pilar de Comunicação.
 * Recebe exclusivamente o contexto do cliente do Maestro (CRMCockpitView)
 * para amarrar os chats e a agenda ao cliente focado no Palco Central.
 */
export interface CockpitSidebarRightProps {
    /** O cliente atualmente focado na visão central. */
    client: Client | null;
}

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * 💬 PILAR 3: COCKPIT SIDEBAR RIGHT (O Centro Omnichannel)
 * @description Gerencia a comunicação fluida com o cliente (WhatsApp, Telegram, Agenda).
 * * 🏆 PADRÃO DE QUALIDADE CLASSE OURO (Colapsável e Reativo):
 * - Funciona de forma autônoma: Controla sua própria expansão (width) sem 
 * forçar re-renderizações no Maestro (CockpitView).
 * - Utiliza flexbox com larguras fixas durante a animação para evitar 
 * "esmagamento" (squishing) do conteúdo enquanto a barra desliza.
 * ============================================================================
 */
export const CockpitSidebarRight: React.FC<CockpitSidebarRightProps> = ({ client }) => {
    
    // Estado local para controle da gaveta (Retirado do Maestro e isolado aqui)
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<OmnichannelTab>(null);

    // Derivação de estado para controle visual
    const isExpanded = activeTab !== null;

    /**
     * @method handleTabToggle
     * @description Alternador inteligente. Abre a aba solicitada, ou fecha a gaveta
     * se o usuário clicar no ícone que já está ativo.
     */
    const handleTabToggle = (tab: OmnichannelTab) => {
        setActiveTab(prev => prev === tab ? null : tab);
    };

    return (
        <aside 
            className={`
                flex-shrink-0 flex flex-row h-full bg-gray-900 border-l border-gray-800 z-20 
                shadow-[-4px_0_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.2)] overflow-hidden
                transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out
                ${isExpanded ? 'w-[380px]' : 'w-16'}
            `}
        >
            {/* ================================================================
                A DOCK (Tira Vertical de Ícones Fixa)
            ================================================================ */}
            <div className="w-16 flex-shrink-0 h-full bg-gray-900 border-r border-gray-800 flex flex-col items-center pt-4 pb-4 gap-4 z-30">
                
                {/* Botão: WhatsApp */}
                <button 
                    onClick={() => handleTabToggle('WA')}
                    className={`
                        w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all shadow-sm
                        ${activeTab === 'WA' 
                            ? 'bg-green-900/40 text-green-400 border border-green-500/50 shadow-green-900/50 scale-110' 
                            : 'bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 text-gray-500 hover:text-green-500 hover:border-green-500/50'
                        }
                    `}
                    title="WhatsApp"
                >
                    <FaWhatsapp size={20} />
                </button>

                {/* Botão: Telegram */}
                <button 
                    onClick={() => handleTabToggle('TG')}
                    className={`
                        w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all shadow-sm
                        ${activeTab === 'TG' 
                            ? 'bg-blue-900/40 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/50 shadow-blue-900/50 scale-110' 
                            : 'bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 text-gray-500 hover:text-blue-500 hover:border-blue-500/50'
                        }
                    `}
                    title="Telegram"
                >
                    <FaTelegram size={18} />
                </button>

                {/* Linha Divisória */}
                <div className="w-8 border-b border-gray-700/50 rounded-full my-1"></div>

                {/* Botão: Agenda */}
                <button 
                    onClick={() => handleTabToggle('AGENDA')}
                    className={`
                        w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all shadow-sm
                        ${activeTab === 'AGENDA' 
                            ? 'bg-accent-primary/20 text-accent-primary border border-accent-primary/50 shadow-orange-900/50 scale-110' 
                            : 'bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 text-gray-500 hover:text-accent-primary hover:border-accent-primary/50'
                        }
                    `}
                    title="Agenda e Compromissos"
                >
                    <FaCalendarAlt size={16} />
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* ================================================================
                ÁREA DE CONTEÚDO (O Painel que Desliza)
                Mantemos a largura fixa (w-[316px] -> 380px total - 16px da dock)
                para evitar esmagamento do layout durante a transição do CSS.
            ================================================================ */}
            <div className="w-[316px] h-full flex flex-col bg-base-metallic relative">
                
                {/* --- HEADER DO PAINEL --- */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/80">
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-wide uppercase flex items-center gap-2">
                        {activeTab === 'WA' && <><FaWhatsapp className="text-green-400" /> WhatsApp</>}
                        {activeTab === 'TG' && <><FaTelegram className="text-blue-400" /> Telegram</>}
                        {activeTab === 'AGENDA' && <><FaCalendarAlt className="text-accent-primary" /> Agenda</>}
                    </h3>
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab(null)} 
                        className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-700"
                        title="Recolher Painel"
                    >
                        <FaTimes size={14} />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* --- CORPO DOS WIDGETS --- */}
                <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden relative bg-gray-900/50">
                    
                    {/* Widget: WhatsApp */}
                    {activeTab === 'WA' && <WhatsAppWidget />}
                    
                    {/* Widget: Telegram (Em Breve) */}
                    {activeTab === 'TG' && (
                        <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-600 opacity-60 p-4 animate-fade-in">
                            <FaTelegram className="w-12 h-12 mb-3" />
                            <p className="text-xs text-center font-medium">Integração com Telegram em desenvolvimento.</p>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Widget: Agenda Compacta */}
                    {activeTab === 'AGENDA' && (
                        <div className="h-full p-2 bg-gray-800 animate-fade-in">
                            <SchedulerWidget 
                                mode="MINI" 
                                clientId={client?.id} // Conecta a agenda ao cliente ativo no Palco Central
                            />
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>

                {/* --- FOOTER: INPUT DE MENSAGEM (Oculto na Agenda) --- */}
                {activeTab !== 'AGENDA' && (
                    <div className="p-3 border-t border-gray-700 bg-gray-800">
                        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                            <input 
                                type="text" 
                                placeholder={client ? `Mensagem para ${client.name.split(' ')[0]}...` : "Selecione um cliente..."}
                                className="flex-1 bg-gray-900 text-xs text-white px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-gray-500 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
                                disabled={!client}
                            />
                            <button className="p-2.5 bg-gray-700 text-gray-400 rounded-lg cursor-not-allowed hover:bg-gray-600 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
                                <FaPaperPlane className="w-3 h-3" />
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </aside>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/presentation/components/cockpit/client-dashboard/ClientDashboard.tsx =====

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
    FaUser, FaEdit, FaEnvelope, FaPhone, FaMapMarkerAlt, 
    FaIdCard, FaBrain, FaSave, FaTimes, 
    FaMagic, FaPlusCircle, FaStickyNote, 
    FaChevronDown, FaChevronUp
} from 'react-icons/fa';
import { Client, AddressType } from '@/core/client/types/client';

// --- AQUI ESTÁ A MUDANÇA: Importamos o Módulo Soberano ---
import { TimelineModule } from '@/modules/timeline/TimelineModule';
import { AppModules } from '@/core/ai/types/enums/AIEnums';
import { ClientForm } from './ClientForm';

export type DashboardMode = 'VIEW' | 'EDIT' | 'CREATE';

interface ClientDashboardProps {
    client: Client | null;
    mode: DashboardMode;
    onModeChange: (mode: DashboardMode) => void;
    onUpdateSuccess: () => void;
}

type DashboardTab = 'OVERVIEW' | 'TIMELINE';

// --- SUB-COMPONENTE: CARD EXPANSÍVEL (Mantido igual) ---
const ExpandableInfoCard: React.FC<{ 
    title: string; 
    icon: React.ElementType; 
    content: string | null; 
    placeholder: string;
}> = ({ title, icon: Icon, content, placeholder }) => {
    
    const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);
    const shouldCollapse = content && content.length > 200;

    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-800/50 rounded-xl border border-gray-600 p-4 relative overflow-hidden group transition-all hover:border-gray-500">
            <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-1 h-full bg-gradient-to-b from-accent-secondary to-accent-primary"></div>
            
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white flex items-center">
                    <Icon className="mr-2 text-accent-primary" /> {title}
                </h3>
            </div>
            
            <div className="relative">
                <div className={`text-sm text-gray-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap ${!isExpanded && shouldCollapse ? 'line-clamp-3' : ''}`}>
                    {content || <span className="text-gray-500 italic">{placeholder}</span>}
                </div>

                {shouldCollapse && (
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
                        className="mt-2 text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-accent-secondary hover:text-white flex items-center transition focus:outline-none"
                    >
                        {isExpanded ? (
                            <>Recolher <FaChevronUp className="ml-1" /></>
                        ) : (
                            <>Ler mais <FaChevronDown className="ml-1" /></>
                        )}
                    </button>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

/**
 * @component ClientDashboard
 * @description Painel Central Unificado.
 * @update Integrado com o novo TimelineModule V8.
 */
export const ClientDashboard: React.FC<ClientDashboardProps> = ({ 
    client, 
    mode, 
    onModeChange, 
    onUpdateSuccess 
}) => {
    
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<DashboardTab>('OVERVIEW');

    // Reset da tab quando muda o cliente
    useEffect(() => {
        if (mode !== 'CREATE') {
            setActiveTab('OVERVIEW');
        }
    }, [client?.id, mode]);

    // Tela de "Nenhum Cliente Selecionado"
    if (!client && mode !== 'CREATE') {
        return (
            <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-500 bg-base-metallic/50 rounded-xl border border-gray-700/50 border-dashed">
                <div className="p-4 bg-gray-800 rounded-full mb-4">
                    <FaUser className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-600" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-400">Nenhum Cliente Selecionado</h3>
                <p className="text-sm mt-2 max-w-xs text-center opacity-70">
                    Selecione um cliente na lista à esquerda ou clique em "Novo" para cadastrar.
                </p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    const isCreating = mode === 'CREATE';
    const isFormOpen = mode === 'CREATE' || mode === 'EDIT';

    // Objeto de fallback seguro para renderização
    const displayClient = client || {
        name: 'Novo Cliente',
        document: '',
        email: '',
        phone: '',
        isActive: true,
        aiSummary: null,
        userNotes: null,
        addresses: []
    } as unknown as Client;

    const mainAddress = displayClient.addresses?.find(a => a.type === AddressType.MAIN) || displayClient.addresses?.[0];
    const addressText = mainAddress 
        ? `${mainAddress.street}, ${mainAddress.number} - ${mainAddress.district}, ${mainAddress.city}/${mainAddress.state}`
        : 'Sem endereço cadastrado';

    return (
        <div className="bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden shadow-2xl relative">
            
            {/* --- HEADER (Fixo) --- */}
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-800 to-gray-900 flex-shrink-0">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
                        <div className={`h-16 w-16 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 shadow-lg ${isCreating ? 'bg-gray-700 border-gray-500 text-gray-400' : 'bg-accent-primary/20 border-accent-primary text-accent-primary'}`}>
                            {isCreating ? (
                                <FaPlusCircle className="text-2xl" />
                            ) : (
                                <span className="text-2xl font-bold">{displayClient.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</span>
                            )}
                        </div>
                        
                        <div>
                            <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white tracking-tight">
                                {isCreating ? 'Cadastrando Novo Cliente' : displayClient.name}
                            </h1>
                            
                            {!isCreating && (
                                <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 mt-1 text-sm text-gray-400">
                                    {displayClient.document ? (
                                        <span className="flex items-center"><FaIdCard className="mr-1.5 opacity-70" /> {displayClient.document}</span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="flex items-center text-accent-secondary"><FaMagic className="mr-1.5" /> Lead (Em prospecção)</span>
                                    )}
                                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] uppercase border ${displayClient.isActive ? 'border-green-500/30 text-green-400 bg-green-500/10' : 'border-red-500/30 text-red-400 bg-red-500/10'}`}>
                                        {displayClient.isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex space-x-2">
                        {isFormOpen ? (
                            <>
                                <button 
                                    onClick={() => onModeChange('VIEW')}
                                    className="flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-gray-300 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 rounded-lg transition text-xs font-bold border border-gray-600"
                                >
                                    <FaTimes className="mr-1" /> Cancelar
                                </button>
                                <button 
                                    type="submit"
                                    form="client-edit-form"
                                    className="flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-white bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 rounded-lg transition text-xs font-bold shadow-lg"
                                >
                                    <FaSave className="mr-1" /> {isCreating ? 'Cadastrar' : 'Salvar'}
                                </button>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <button 
                                onClick={() => onModeChange('EDIT')}
                                className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-white bg-gray-700/50 hover:bg-gray-700 rounded-lg transition border border-gray-600"
                                title="Editar Dados"
                            >
                                <FaEdit className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* --- NAVEGAÇÃO (Abas) --- */}
            {!isFormOpen && (
                <div className="flex border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 px-6 flex-shrink-0">
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('OVERVIEW')}
                        className={`py-3 px-4 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 transition ${activeTab === 'OVERVIEW' ? 'border-accent-primary text-white' : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
                    >
                        Visão Geral & IA
                    </button>
                    <button 
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab('TIMELINE')}
                        className={`py-3 px-4 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 transition ${activeTab === 'TIMELINE' ? 'border-accent-primary text-white' : 'border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
                    >
                        Timeline (Histórico)
                    </button>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* --- ÁREA DE CONTEÚDO PRINCIPAL --- 
                Lógica de Scroll Inteligente:
                - Se for OVERVIEW ou FORM: Usa overflow-y-auto (scroll da página)
                - Se for TIMELINE: Usa overflow-hidden (o scroll é interno do módulo timeline)
            */}
            <div className={`
                flex-1 
                ${activeTab === 'TIMELINE' && !isFormOpen ? 'overflow-hidden' : 'overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-6'}
            `}>
                
                {isFormOpen ? (
                    // --- MODO FORMULÁRIO (EDIT/CREATE) ---
                    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in pb-6">
                        <div className="opacity-60 grayscale cursor-not-allowed">
                            <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                title="Dossiê Vivo (IA Memory)"
                                icon={FaBrain}
                                content={displayClient.aiSummary}
                                placeholder="O dossiê será gerado automaticamente."
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-gray-700">
                            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-accent-secondary uppercase mb-4">
                                {isCreating ? 'Preencha os dados do novo cliente' : 'Editando Dados Cadastrais'}
                            </h4>
                            <ClientForm 
                                client={isCreating ? undefined : client!} 
                                onSuccess={onUpdateSuccess}
                                showSubmitButton={false}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    // --- MODO VISUALIZAÇÃO ---
                    <>
                        {activeTab === 'OVERVIEW' && (
                            <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
                                <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                    title="Dossiê Vivo (IA Memory)"
                                    icon={FaBrain}
                                    content={displayClient.aiSummary}
                                    placeholder="Ainda não há um resumo gerado pela Inteligência Artificial."
                                />
                                <ExpandableInfoCard 
                                    title="Notas Pessoais"
                                    icon={FaStickyNote}
                                    content={displayClient.userNotes}
                                    placeholder="Nenhuma nota privada adicionada. Clique em editar para adicionar."
                                />

                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                    <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
                                        <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-3">Contato</h4>
                                        <div className="space-y-3">
                                            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-gray-200">
                                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400"><FaEnvelope /></div>
                                                <span className="truncate">{displayClient.email || 'Não informado'}</span>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-gray-200">
                                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400"><FaPhone /></div>
                                                <span>{displayClient.phone || 'Não informado'}</span>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="bg-gray-800 rounded-lg p-4 border border-gray-700">
                                        <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-3">Localização Principal</h4>
                                        <div className="flex items-start text-sm text-gray-200">
                                            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-3 text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0"><FaMapMarkerAlt /></div>
                                            <span className="leading-relaxed">{addressText}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {activeTab === 'TIMELINE' && (
                            /* MÓDULO SOBERANO TIMELINE 
                                - Ocupa 100% da altura disponível (h-full)
                                - entityId pode ser undefined se o cliente for null (embora o 'if' lá em cima proteja)
                            */
                            <div className="h-full w-full animate-fade-in">
                                <TimelineModule 
                                    entityId={displayClient.id} 
                                    module={AppModules.CLIENTE}
                                    className="h-full" // Garante que o scroll interno funcione
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/crm/presentation/components/cockpit/client-dashboard/ClientForm.tsx =====

// ===== FILE: src/core/components/client/ClientForm.tsx =====

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { FaMapMarkerAlt, FaExclamationTriangle, FaSave, FaStickyNote } from 'react-icons/fa';

import { useClientMutation } from '@/core/client/hooks/useClientMutation';
import { Client, AddressType } from '@/core/client/types/client';
import { ClientFormSchema, ClientFormInput } from '@/core/client/schemas/clientSchema';

interface ClientFormProps {
    client?: Client;
    onSuccess: (savedClient: Client) => void;
    showSubmitButton?: boolean;
}

/**
 * @component ClientForm
 * @description Formulário Headless (Puro) de Cliente.
 * * 🏆 PADRÃO DE QUALIDADE CLASSE OURO:
 * 1. Single Source of Truth: Consome o `ClientFormSchema` (Zod) garantindo que as regras
 * de validação do CRM sejam matematicamente idênticas às da tela de Master Data.
 * 2. UX de Resiliência (Tratamento 409): Intercepta erros de "Documento Duplicado" 
 * e acopla o erro diretamente no input do usuário, em vez de um alerta genérico.
 * 3. HTML5 Nativo: Permite submissão externa via `form="client-edit-form"`, viabilizando
 * layouts complexos como a Workstation do CRM.
 */
export const ClientForm: React.FC<ClientFormProps> = ({ 
    client, 
    onSuccess, 
    showSubmitButton = true 
}) => {
    
    const { createClient, updateClient, isLoading } = useClientMutation();
    const isEditing = !!client;
    
    // Estado para erros genéricos (ex: 500 do servidor)
    const [globalError, setGlobalError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    // =========================================================================
    // 🧠 MOTOR DO FORMULÁRIO (RHF + Zod)
    // =========================================================================
    const {
        register,
        handleSubmit,
        reset,
        setError,
        formState: { errors }
    } = useForm<ClientFormInput & { address?: any }>({
        resolver: zodResolver(ClientFormSchema as any),
        mode: 'onChange'
    });

    // =========================================================================
    // 💧 HIDRATAÇÃO DOS DADOS
    // =========================================================================
    useEffect(() => {
        if (client) {
            // Busca o endereço principal (se houver) para popular os campos
            const mainAddress = client.addresses?.find(a => a.type === AddressType.MAIN) || client.addresses?.[0];
            
            reset({
                id: client.id,
                name: client.name,
                document: client.document || '',
                email: client.email || '',
                phone: client.phone || '',
                userNotes: client.userNotes || '',
                isActive: client.isActive,
                address: {
                    id: mainAddress?.id || '',
                    street: mainAddress?.street || '',
                    number: mainAddress?.number || '',
                    district: mainAddress?.district || '',
                    city: mainAddress?.city || '',
                    state: mainAddress?.state || '',
                    zipCode: mainAddress?.zipCode || ''
                }
            });
        } else {
            // Estado virgem para criação
            reset({
                name: '',
                document: '',
                email: '',
                phone: '',
                userNotes: '',
                isActive: true,
                address: { street: '', number: '', district: '', city: '', state: '', zipCode: '' }
            });
        }
    }, [client, reset]);

    // =========================================================================
    // ⚙️ HANDLER DE SUBMISSÃO E TRATAMENTO DE ERROS
    // =========================================================================
    const onSubmitForm = async (data: any) => {
        setGlobalError(null);

        // Montagem do Payload (Mapeia o form plano para o DTO complexo com array de endereços)
        const addressesToSend = data.address?.street?.trim() ? [{
            id: data.address.id || undefined,
            street: data.address.street,
            number: data.address.number || 'S/N',
            district: data.address.district || 'Centro',
            city: data.address.city || 'Indefinida',
            state: data.address.state || 'UF',
            zipCode: data.address.zipCode || '00000000',
            type: AddressType.MAIN
        }] : undefined;

        const payload = {
            name: data.name,
            document: data.document?.trim() || undefined,
            email: data.email?.trim() || undefined,
            phone: data.phone?.trim() || undefined,
            userNotes: data.userNotes?.trim() || undefined,
            isActive: data.isActive,
            addresses: addressesToSend
        };

        try {
            let result: Client;
            if (isEditing && client) {
                result = await updateClient(client.id, payload);
            } else {
                result = await createClient(payload);
            }
            onSuccess(result);
            
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error("❌ [ClientForm] Erro ao salvar:", err);
            
            // 🎯 AQUI RESOLVEMOS A BOMBINHA (Erro 409 - Conflito de Documento/Email)
            if (err.response?.status === 409) {
                const apiMsg = err.response?.data?.message || '';
                
                if (apiMsg.toLowerCase().includes('documento') || apiMsg.toLowerCase().includes('cpf')) {
                    setError('document', { type: 'manual', message: 'Este documento já está cadastrado no sistema.' });
                } else if (apiMsg.toLowerCase().includes('email')) {
                    setError('email', { type: 'manual', message: 'Este e-mail já pertence a outro cliente.' });
                } else {
                    setGlobalError(apiMsg || 'Conflito de dados (registro duplicado).');
                }
            } else {
                setGlobalError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Ocorreu um erro interno ao salvar o cliente.');
            }
        }
    };

    // =========================================================================
    // 🎨 RENDERIZAÇÃO UX/UI
    // =========================================================================
    return (
        <form id="client-edit-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmitForm)} className="space-y-5 animate-fade-in">
            
            {globalError && (
                <div className="p-3 bg-red-900/30 border border-red-800 text-red-300 rounded-lg text-sm flex items-center shadow-inner">
                    <FaExclamationTriangle className="mr-2 flex-shrink-0" />
                    <span>{globalError}</span>
                </div>
            )}

            <div className="space-y-4">
                {/* --- NOME --- */}
                <div>
                    <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-accent-primary uppercase mb-1 tracking-wider">
                        Nome Completo *
                    </label>
                    <input 
                        type="text" 
                        {...register('name')}
                        disabled={isLoading}
                        className={`w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-900 border rounded-lg text-white text-sm focus:outline-none transition placeholder-gray-600 ${errors.name ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-red-500' : 'border-gray-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-secondary focus:border-accent-secondary'}`}
                        placeholder="Ex: João Silva ou Empresa Ltda"
                    />
                    {errors.name && <p className="text-[10px] text-red-400 mt-1">{errors.name.message as string}</p>}
                </div>

                {/* --- CONTATO (Grid) --- */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-1 tracking-wider">
                            E-mail
                        </label>
                        <input 
                            type="email" 
                            {...register('email')}
                            disabled={isLoading}
                            className={`w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-900 border rounded-lg text-white text-sm focus:outline-none transition ${errors.email ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-red-500' : 'border-gray-600 focus:border-gray-400'}`}
                            placeholder="contato@email.com"
                        />
                        {errors.email && <p className="text-[10px] text-red-400 mt-1">{errors.email.message as string}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-1 tracking-wider">
                            Telefone
                        </label>
                        <input 
                            type="text" 
                            {...register('phone')}
                            disabled={isLoading}
                            placeholder="(00) 00000-0000"
                            className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg text-white text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-gray-400 transition" 
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* --- ENDEREÇO PRINCIPAL --- */}
            <div className="bg-gray-800/40 p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-700/50">
                <label className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase mb-3 flex items-center tracking-wider">
                    <FaMapMarkerAlt className="mr-1.5 text-accent-secondary" /> Endereço Principal
                </label>
                
                <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-3">
                    <div className="col-span-3">
                        <input type="text" {...register('address.street')} placeholder="Rua / Logradouro" disabled={isLoading}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none transition-colors" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-1">
                        <input type="text" {...register('address.number')} placeholder="Nº" disabled={isLoading}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none transition-colors" />
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="col-span-2">
                        <input type="text" {...register('address.district')} placeholder="Bairro" disabled={isLoading}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none transition-colors" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-2">
                        <input type="text" {...register('address.city')} placeholder="Cidade" disabled={isLoading}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none transition-colors" />
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="col-span-1">
                        <input type="text" {...register('address.state')} placeholder="UF" maxLength={2} disabled={isLoading}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-2 text-xs text-white uppercase focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none transition-colors" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-3">
                        <input type="text" {...register('address.zipCode')} placeholder="CEP" disabled={isLoading}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 rounded px-2 py-2 text-xs text-white focus:border-accent-secondary focus:outline-none transition-colors" />
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* --- DOCUMENTO (CPF/CNPJ) --- */}
            <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1 tracking-wider">
                    CPF / CNPJ
                </label>
                <input 
                    type="text" 
                    {...register('document')}
                    disabled={isLoading}
                    className={`w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-900 border rounded-lg text-white text-sm font-mono focus:outline-none transition placeholder-gray-600 ${errors.document ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-red-500' : 'border-gray-600 focus:border-gray-400'}`}
                    placeholder="000.000.000-00"
                />
                {errors.document && <p className="text-[10px] text-red-400 mt-1 font-bold animate-pulse">{errors.document.message as string}</p>}
            </div>

            {/* --- ANOTAÇÕES HUMANAS --- */}
            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-gray-700/50">
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2 tracking-wider flex items-center">
                    <FaStickyNote className="mr-1.5 text-yellow-500/70" /> Notas Privadas (Não lidas pela IA)
                </label>
                <textarea
                    {...register('userNotes')}
                    disabled={isLoading}
                    rows={4}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-gray-800/50 border border-gray-600 rounded-lg text-white text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-yellow-500/50 transition placeholder-gray-600 resize-y"
                    placeholder="Digite observações internas sobre este cliente..."
                />
            </div>

            {/* BOTÃO INTERNO (Usado apenas se o modal pai não usar form="client-edit-form") */}
            {showSubmitButton && (
                <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={isLoading}
                    className="w-full flex justify-center items-center py-3 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-lg shadow-lg text-sm font-bold text-white bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 transition disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transform active:scale-95 mt-4"
                >
                    {isLoading ? (
                        <span className="animate-pulse">{isEditing ? 'Salvando...' : 'Cadastrando...'}</span>
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            <FaSave className="mr-2" />
                            {isEditing ? 'Salvar Alterações' : 'Cadastrar Cliente'}
                        </>
                    )}
                </button>
            )}
        </form>
    );
};

// src/modules/crm/components/kanban/DealCard.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { Draggable } from '@hello-pangea/dnd';
import { CRMDeal } from '@/modules/crm/domain/types';
import { FaBuilding } from 'react-icons/fa';

interface DealCardProps {
    deal: CRMDeal;
    index: number;
    onEdit: (deal: CRMDeal) => void; // <--- NOVO
}

export const DealCard: React.FC<DealCardProps> = ({ deal, index, onEdit }) => {
    
    const formattedValue = new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', {
        style: 'currency',
        currency: 'BRL'
    }).format(Number(deal.value));

    return (
        <Draggable draggableId={deal.id} index={index}>
            {(provided, snapshot) => (
                <div
                    ref={provided.innerRef}
                    {...provided.draggableProps}
                    {...provided.dragHandleProps}
                    // --- AÇÃO DE CLIQUE DUPLO PARA EDITAR ---
                    onDoubleClick={() => onEdit(deal)} 
                    className={`
                        bg-gray-800 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-700 shadow-sm mb-3
                        hover:border-accent-secondary/50 transition-all group cursor-pointer
                        ${snapshot.isDragging ? 'shadow-2xl ring-2 ring-accent-primary rotate-2 z-50' : ''}
                    `}
                    style={{
                        ...provided.draggableProps.style,
                    }}
                    title="Clique duas vezes para editar"
                >
                    {/* Título e Probabilidade */}
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white line-clamp-2 leading-tight">
                            {deal.title}
                        </h4>
                        {deal.probability > 0 && (
                            <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded 
                                ${deal.probability >= 70 ? 'bg-green-900/50 text-green-400' : 'bg-yellow-900/50 text-yellow-400'}
                            `}>
                                {deal.probability}%
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Cliente */}
                    <div className="flex items-center text-xs text-gray-400 mb-3">
                        <FaBuilding className="mr-1.5 w-3 h-3 opacity-50" />
                        <span className="truncate max-w-[150px]">
                            {deal.client?.name || 'Cliente Desconhecido'}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Footer */}
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-t border-gray-700/50 pt-2">
                        <div className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-200 flex items-center">
                            <span className="text-green-500 mr-1">$</span> {formattedValue}
                        </div>
                        
                        <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] text-white border border-gray-600">
                            <span className="font-bold">V</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </Draggable>
    );
};
