// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/presentation/containers/ProductContainer.tsx =====

'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
    FaChartPie, 
    FaBox, 
    FaExchangeAlt, 
    FaWarehouse, 
    FaTags, 
    FaFileInvoiceDollar 
} from 'react-icons/fa';

// --- Imports de Componentes Locais (Módulo Products) ---
import { OverviewTab } from '../components/tabs/OverviewTab';

// --- Imports Cross-Module (Reutilização de Componentes Existentes) ---
import { useAIContextInjector } from '@/core/ai/hooks/useAIContextInjector';
import { FeatureKeys } from '@/core/ai/types/enums/AIEnums';
import { CategoryRegistry } from '../registries/CategoryRegistry';
import { FiscalRegistry } from '@/modules/fiscal/registries/FiscalRegistry';
import { ProductRegistry } from '../registries/ProductRegistry';
import { WarehouseRegistry } from '@/modules/inventory/registries/WarehouseRegistry';
import { InventoryRegistry } from '@/modules/inventory/registries/InventoryRegistry';

export type ProductViewType = 
    | 'OVERVIEW'
    | 'CATALOG'
    | 'MOVEMENTS'
    | 'WAREHOUSES'
    | 'CATEGORIES'
    | 'FISCAL';

interface ProductContainerProps {
    initialView?: ProductViewType;
}

/**
 * @component ProductContainer
 * @description Hub Principal de Supply Chain.
 * @style Dark/Metallic (Seguindo Design System)
 */
export const ProductContainer: React.FC<ProductContainerProps> = ({ 
    initialView = 'CATALOG' 
}) => {
    
    useAIContextInjector(FeatureKeys.AI_INVENTORY_MICRO);
    
    const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState<ProductViewType>(initialView);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (initialView) {
            setActiveView(initialView);
        }
    }, [initialView]);

    const tabs = [
        { id: 'OVERVIEW', label: 'Visão Geral', icon: FaChartPie },
        { id: 'CATALOG', label: 'Catálogo', icon: FaBox },
        { id: 'MOVEMENTS', label: 'Movimentações', icon: FaExchangeAlt },
        { id: 'WAREHOUSES', label: 'Depósitos', icon: FaWarehouse },
        { id: 'CATEGORIES', label: 'Categorias', icon: FaTags },
        { id: 'FISCAL', label: 'Fiscal', icon: FaFileInvoiceDollar },
    ];

    const renderContent = () => {
        switch (activeView) {
            case 'OVERVIEW':
                return <OverviewTab />;
            case 'CATALOG':
                return <ProductRegistry />;
            case 'MOVEMENTS':
                return <InventoryRegistry />;
            case 'WAREHOUSES':
                return <WarehouseRegistry />;
            case 'CATEGORIES':
                // Nova implementação: Componente nativo do módulo Products
                return <CategoryRegistry />;
            case 'FISCAL':
                return <FiscalRegistry />
            default:
                return <OverviewTab />;
        }
    };

    return (
        // BASE: Fundo Escuro (#111827) e Texto Claro (#F3F4F6)
        <div className="flex flex-col h-full w-full bg-base-dark text-base-text">
            
            {/* HEADER: Fundo Escuro com Borda Sutil para separar do conteúdo */}
            <header className="bg-base-dark border-b border-gray-800 px-8 pt-8 pb-0 sticky top-0 z-20">
                <div className="flex flex-col space-y-6">
                    
                    {/* Título e Ações */}
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center pb-2">
                        <div>
                            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-base-text tracking-tight animate-fade-in-down">
                                {activeView === 'OVERVIEW' ? 'Supply Chain Tower' : 'Gestão de Materiais'}
                            </h1>
                            <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1 animate-fade-in">
                                {activeView === 'OVERVIEW' 
                                    ? 'Monitoramento estratégico de estoque e movimentações.' 
                                    : 'Controle operacional e cadastros de produtos.'}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Navegação de Abas */}
                    <nav className="flex space-x-8 overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar">
                        {tabs.map((tab) => {
                            const isActive = activeView === tab.id;
                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={tab.id}
                                    onClick={() => setActiveView(tab.id as ProductViewType)}
                                    className={`
                                        group flex items-center space-x-2 py-4 px-1 border-b-2 font-medium text-sm transition-all duration-200 whitespace-nowrap
                                        ${isActive 
                                            ? 'border-accent-primary text-accent-primary' // Ativo: Laranja Vibrante
                                            : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-base-text hover:border-gray-700' // Inativo: Cinza -> Claro
                                        }
                                    `}
                                >
                                    <tab.icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${isActive ? 'text-accent-primary' : 'text-gray-500 group-hover:text-gray-300'}`} />
                                    <span>{tab.label}</span>
                                </button>
                            );
                        })}
                    </nav>
                </div>
            </header>

            {/* MAIN: Conteúdo com Fundo Transparente (herda base-dark) */}
            <main className="flex-1 p-8 overflow-y-auto animate-fade-in custom-scrollbar">
                {renderContent()}
            </main>

        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/presentation/registries/CategoryRegistry.tsx =====

// ===== FILE: src/modules/categories/registries/CategoryRegistry.tsx =====

'use client';

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { z } from 'zod';

import { CrudLayout } from '@/core/components/crud/CrudLayout';
import { CrudConfig, CrudHooks } from '@/core/components/crud/types';
import { useCategories } from '@/modules/categories/hooks/useCategories';
import { useCategoryMutation } from '@/modules/categories/hooks/useCategoryMutation';
import { Category, CategoryType } from '@/modules/categories/types';

// =========================================================================
// 1. A PONTE DE TRADUÇÃO (Zod Schema)
// =========================================================================
const CategoryFormSchema = z.object({
    name: z.string().min(2, 'O nome deve ter no mínimo 2 caracteres'),
    
    // O select HTML envia string vazia (''). Nós a aceitamos pacientemente.
    parentId: z.uuid('UUID inválido').optional().or(z.literal('')), 
    
    // 🎚️ Usando um Select limpo e direto como você sugeriu!
    // 🎚️ Zod v4: z.enum agora lê Enums nativos sozinho e usa a propriedade unificada 'error'
    type: z.enum(CategoryType, { 
        error: 'Selecione um tipo de categoria válido' 
    }),
    
    isActive: z.boolean().default(true),

    // 🛡️ Campos de Auditoria
    id: z.string().optional(),
    slug: z.string().optional(),
    createdAt: z.string().optional(),
    updatedAt: z.string().optional(),

}).transform((data) => ({
    ...data,
    // TRADUÇÃO DE SAÍDA: O backend não aceita string vazia, ele quer 'undefined'.
    parentId: data.parentId === '' ? undefined : data.parentId,
    attributesSchema: [] // Garante o JSONB pro Backend
}));

type CategoryFormData = z.input<typeof CategoryFormSchema>;

// =========================================================================
// 2. O COMPONENTE MESTRE
// =========================================================================
export const CategoryRegistry: React.FC = () => {
    
    const { categories, isLoading, error: queryError, refreshCategories } = useCategories();
    const { createCategory, updateCategory, deleteCategory, error: mutationError } = useCategoryMutation();

    // =========================================================================
    // 🔄 FILTRO DE HIDRATAÇÃO (O Fim do Erro Null)
    // O Banco manda 'null', mas a UI só entende string vazia ('').
    // Interceptamos aqui para limpar os dados antes de chegarem na tela.
    // =========================================================================
    const safeCategories = useMemo(() => {
        return (categories || []).map(cat => ({
            ...cat,
            // Substitui null por string vazia de forma segura
            parentId: cat.parentId === null ? '' : cat.parentId
        }));
    }, [categories]);

    // Usamos as categorias seguras para montar o Dropdown
    const parentOptions = useMemo(() => {
        return safeCategories.map(cat => ({
            label: cat.name,
            value: cat.id
        }));
    }, [safeCategories]);

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. A PLANTA DA TELA (Configuração)
    // =========================================================================
    const categoryConfig: CrudConfig<Category, CategoryFormData> = useMemo(() => ({
        title: 'Categorias',
        description: 'Organize o catálogo de produtos e serviços do sistema.',
        schema: CategoryFormSchema as any,
        
        columns: [
            { header: 'Nome da Categoria', accessor: 'name' },
            { 
                header: 'Tipo', 
                accessor: (item) => (
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] uppercase font-bold border ${
                        item.type === CategoryType.PRODUCT ? 'border-blue-500/30 text-blue-400 bg-blue-500/10' : 'border-purple-500/30 text-purple-400 bg-purple-500/10'
                    }`}>
                        {item.type === CategoryType.PRODUCT ? 'Produto' : 'Serviço'}
                    </span>
                )
            },
            { 
                header: 'Status', 
                accessor: (item) => (
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] uppercase font-bold border ${
                        item.isActive ? 'border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/10' : 'border-gray-500/30 text-gray-400 bg-gray-500/10'
                    }`}>
                        {item.isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}
                    </span>
                )
            },
        ],
        
        fields: [
            { 
                name: 'name', 
                label: 'Nome da Categoria', 
                type: 'text', 
                placeholder: 'Ex: Eletrônicos, Consultoria...' 
            },
            { 
                name: 'type', 
                label: 'Tipo de Categoria', 
                type: 'select', // 👈 Solução limpa e nativa!
                options: [
                    { label: 'Produto Físico/Digital', value: CategoryType.PRODUCT },
                    { label: 'Serviço Prestado', value: CategoryType.SERVICE }
                ]
            },
            {
                name: 'parentId',
                label: 'Categoria Principal (Pai)',
                type: 'select',
                options: parentOptions, 
                disabled: isLoading
            },
            { 
                name: 'isActive', 
                label: 'Status no Sistema', 
                type: 'boolean' // Voltou a ser o padrão (Ativo/Inativo)
            }
        ],

        systemFields: [
            { name: 'id', label: 'ID do Registro (UUID)', type: 'text' },
            { name: 'slug', label: 'Slug (URL Amigável)', type: 'text' },
            { name: 'createdAt', label: 'Data de Criação', type: 'text' },
            { name: 'updatedAt', label: 'Última Modificação', type: 'text' }
        ]

    }), [parentOptions, isLoading]);

    const crudHooks: CrudHooks<Category, CategoryFormData> = {
        data: safeCategories, // 👈 O Motor agora consome os dados blindados
        isLoading: isLoading,
        error: queryError || mutationError || null,
        onCreate: createCategory as any,
        onUpdate: updateCategory as any,
        onDelete: deleteCategory,
        onRefresh: refreshCategories
    };

    return <CrudLayout<Category, CategoryFormData> config={categoryConfig} hooks={crudHooks} />;
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/products/presentation/registries/ProductRegistry.tsx =====

'use client';

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { 
    FaBoxOpen, FaCube, FaTruck, FaHome, 
    FaUtensils, FaFileCode, FaCogs 
} from 'react-icons/fa';

// --- Motores do Core CRUD V4 (Arquitetura Master Data) ---
import { CrudLayout } from '@/core/components/crud/CrudLayout';
import { CrudConfig, CrudHooks } from '@/core/components/crud/types';

// --- Inteligência de Domínio (NOVO DIRETÓRIO UNIFICADO - SEM LEGADO) ---
// Importamos apenas o index.ts que criamos nos passos anteriores
import { ProductFormSchema, ProductFormInput } from '../../domain/schemas/productSchema';
import { 
    ProductType, 
    BaseUOM, 
    ProductDomainModel 
} from '../../domain/types/enums';

// --- Hooks de Integração ---
import { useProducts } from '../../application/hooks/useProducts';
import { useProductMutation } from '../../application/hooks/useProductMutation';
import { useCategories } from '@/modules/categories/hooks/useCategories';
import { useFiscal } from '@/modules/fiscal/hooks/useFiscal';

// --- Operários de Metadados (Pele Dinâmica do JSONB) ---
import { VehicleFields } from '../components/metadata-forms/VehicleFields';
import { RealEstateFields } from '../components/metadata-forms/RealEstateFields';
import { DigitalFields } from '../components/metadata-forms/DigitalFields';
import { ServiceFields } from '../components/metadata-forms/ServiceFields';
import { FoodFields } from '../components/metadata-forms/FoodFields';
import { PhysicalFields } from '../components/metadata-forms/PhysicalFields';

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * 🏭 REGISTRY: ProductRegistry
 * @description Maestro principal para gestão de produtos e serviços polimórficos.
 * * 🏆 PADRÃO DE QUALIDADE CLASSE OURO (Zero Legado):
 * 1. Clean Imports: Utiliza exclusivamente a nova estrutura de types/ modular.
 * 2. Type Guarding: Resolve erros 2322 via as-casting seguro no switch-case.
 * 3. Dynamic Injection: Gerencia o JSONB 'metadata' via Custom Render do Motor V4.
 * ============================================================================
 */
export const ProductRegistry: React.FC = () => {
    const { products, isLoading: loadingProducts, error: queryError, refreshProducts } = useProducts();
    const { createProduct, updateProduct, deleteProduct, error: mutationError } = useProductMutation();
    
    const { categories, isLoading: loadingCats } = useCategories();
    const { taxGroups, isLoading: loadingFiscal } = useFiscal();

    /**
     * 🔄 THE GREAT FLATTENER
     * Normaliza os dados para exibição na tabela.
     */
    const flatProducts = useMemo(() => {
        return (products || []).map(product => {
            const mainVariant = (product.variants && product.variants.length > 0) 
                ? product.variants[0] 
                : {} as any;
            
            const metadataRaw = mainVariant.metadata as any;
            const domain = metadataRaw?.domain || ProductDomainModel.PHYSICAL;

            return {
                ...product,
                variantId: mainVariant.id || '',
                sku: mainVariant.sku || '',
                salePrice: mainVariant.salePrice || 0,
                domainModel: domain,
                // O metadata aqui chega plano para o formulário
                metadata: metadataRaw?.data || {}
            };
        });
    }, [products]);

    /**
     * 📐 CONFIGURAÇÃO DO MOTOR V4
     */
    const productConfig: CrudConfig<any, ProductFormInput> = useMemo(() => ({
        title: 'Catálogo de Itens',
        description: 'Gestão integrada de bens e serviços com inteligência de nicho.',
        icon: <FaBoxOpen className="text-accent-primary" />,
        schema: ProductFormSchema as any,

        columns: [
            { header: 'Nome / Item', accessor: 'name' },
            { 
                header: 'SKU', 
                accessor: (item) => <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-accent-secondary">{item.sku || 'N/A'}</span> 
            },
            { 
                header: 'Preço Venda', 
                accessor: (item) => (
                    <span className="font-bold text-green-400">
                        {new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL' }).format(item.salePrice)}
                    </span>
                ) 
            },
            { 
                header: 'Status', 
                accessor: (item) => (
                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold uppercase ${
                        item.isActive ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400' : 'bg-gray-800 text-gray-500'
                    }`}>
                        {item.isActive ? 'Ativo' : 'Inativo'}
                    </span>
                )
            },
        ],

        fields: [
            { name: 'name', label: 'Nome do Produto/Serviço', type: 'text' },
            { 
                name: 'type', 
                label: 'Estrutura Comercial', 
                type: 'select', 
                options: [
                    { label: '📦 Simples', value: ProductType.SIMPLE },
                    { label: '📊 Grade', value: ProductType.VARIABLE },
                    { label: '🛠️ Serviço', value: ProductType.SERVICE },
                ] 
            },
            { 
                name: 'domainModel', 
                label: 'Modelo de Domínio', 
                type: 'select', 
                options: [
                    { label: '🛒 Físico Geral', value: ProductDomainModel.PHYSICAL },
                    { label: '🏎️ Automotivo', value: ProductDomainModel.VEHICLE },
                    { label: '🏠 Imobiliário', value: ProductDomainModel.REAL_ESTATE },
                    { label: '🍕 Alimentação', value: ProductDomainModel.FOOD },
                    { label: '💾 Digital', value: ProductDomainModel.DIGITAL },
                    { label: '⚙️ Serviço Especializado', value: ProductDomainModel.SERVICE },
                ] 
            },
            { name: 'categoryId', label: 'Categoria', type: 'select', options: categories.map(c => ({ label: c.name, value: c.id })), disabled: loadingCats },
            { name: 'taxGroupId', label: 'Regra Fiscal', type: 'select', options: taxGroups.map(t => ({ label: t.name, value: t.id })), disabled: loadingFiscal },
            { name: 'baseUOM', label: 'Unidade de Medida', type: 'select', options: Object.values(BaseUOM).map(v => ({ label: v, value: v })) },
            { name: 'salePrice', label: 'Preço (R$)', type: 'number' },
            { name: 'sku', label: 'SKU Interno', type: 'text' },

            /**
             * 🧠 INJEÇÃO POLIMÓRFICA (Custom Render)
             * Resolve o erro 2322 garantindo que o metadataValue nunca seja undefined.
             */
            {
                name: 'metadata',
                label: 'Metadados Técnicos',
                type: 'custom',
                customRender: (form) => {
                    const domain = form.watch('domainModel') as ProductDomainModel;
                    const metadataValue = form.watch('metadata') || {}; // Garantia de objeto vazio
                    const setMetadata = (data: any) => form.setValue('metadata', data, { shouldValidate: true });

                    return (
                        <div className="col-span-full bg-gray-900/30 p-5 rounded-2xl border border-gray-800 shadow-inner animate-fade-in">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 opacity-50 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">
                                <FaCogs /> Especificações: {domain}
                            </div>

                            {domain === ProductDomainModel.PHYSICAL && <PhysicalFields metadata={metadataValue as any} onChange={setMetadata} />}
                            {domain === ProductDomainModel.VEHICLE && <VehicleFields metadata={metadataValue as any} onChange={setMetadata} />}
                            {domain === ProductDomainModel.REAL_ESTATE && <RealEstateFields metadata={metadataValue as any} onChange={setMetadata} />}
                            {domain === ProductDomainModel.FOOD && <FoodFields metadata={metadataValue as any} onChange={setMetadata} />}
                            {domain === ProductDomainModel.DIGITAL && <DigitalFields metadata={metadataValue as any} onChange={setMetadata} />}
                            {domain === ProductDomainModel.SERVICE && <ServiceFields metadata={metadataValue as any} onChange={setMetadata} />}
                        </div>
                    );
                }
            },
            { name: 'isActive', label: 'Dísponível para venda', type: 'boolean' }
        ],

        systemFields: [
            { name: 'id', label: 'ID Produto', type: 'text' },
            { name: 'createdAt', label: 'Data Cadastro', type: 'text' }
        ]
    }), [categories, taxGroups, loadingCats, loadingFiscal]);

    const crudHooks: CrudHooks<any, ProductFormInput> = {
        data: flatProducts,
        isLoading: loadingProducts,
        error: queryError || mutationError || null,
        onCreate: createProduct as any,
        onUpdate: updateProduct as any,
        onDelete: deleteProduct,
        onRefresh: refreshProducts
    };

    return <CrudLayout config={productConfig} hooks={crudHooks} />;
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/scheduler/components/AppointmentModal.tsx =====

// src/modules/scheduler/components/AppointmentModal.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaTimes, FaCalendarPlus, FaClock, FaAlignLeft, FaTag, FaSave } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

interface AppointmentModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    /**
     * Datas pré-selecionadas ao clicar no calendário (Slot)
     */
    initialSlot?: { start: Date; end: Date };
    currentAppointment?: any; // Recebe o objeto do evento clicado (modo edição)
    /**
     * Callback para salvar (será conectado ao hook no próximo passo)
     */
    onConfirm: (data: { title: string; description: string; start: Date; end: Date }) => Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * @component AppointmentModal
 * @description Modal compacto e elegante para criação rápida de agendamentos.
 * Usa backdrop-blur para não perder o contexto do fundo.
 */
export const AppointmentModal: React.FC<AppointmentModalProps> = ({ 
    isOpen, 
    onClose, 
    initialSlot,
    currentAppointment,
    onConfirm 
}) => {
    
    // --- ESTADOS DO FORMULÁRIO ---
    const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
    const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
    
    // Armazenamos como string para o input type="datetime-local"
    const [startStr, setStartStr] = useState('');
    const [endStr, setEndStr] = useState('');
    
    const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

    // Sincroniza o formulário quando o slot inicial muda
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            if (currentAppointment) {
                // MODO EDIÇÃO: Preenche com dados do agendamento existente
                setTitle(currentAppointment.title);
                setDescription(currentAppointment.description || '');
                
                // Converte as datas (que podem vir como string ISO do banco) para objeto Date
                const startDate = new Date(currentAppointment.startTime);
                const endDate = new Date(currentAppointment.endTime);

                setStartStr(format(startDate, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"));
                setEndStr(format(endDate, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"));
            } 
            else if (initialSlot) {
                // MODO CRIAÇÃO: Preenche com o slot clicado (vazio)
                setStartStr(format(initialSlot.start, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"));
                setEndStr(format(initialSlot.end, "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm"));
                setTitle('');
                setDescription('');
            }
        }
    }, [isOpen, initialSlot, currentAppointment]);

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        
        if (!title.trim() || !startStr || !endStr) return;

        setIsSaving(true);
        try {
            await onConfirm({
                title,
                description,
                start: new Date(startStr),
                end: new Date(endStr)
            });
            onClose();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Erro ao salvar:", error);
            // O tratamento de erro ideal seria um toast/alert aqui
        } finally {
            setIsSaving(false);
        }
    };

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        // OVERLAY: Fundo escuro translúcido com Blur (Suave)
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/20 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 transition-all duration-300">
            
            {/* CONTAINER: Compacto, Sombra Suave, Bordas Arredondadas */}
            <div 
                className="bg-base-metallic w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-700 transform scale-100 transition-all flex flex-col overflow-hidden"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                
                {/* HEADER: Minimalista */}
                <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-700 bg-gray-800/50 flex justify-between items-center">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="p-2 bg-accent-secondary/20 text-accent-secondary rounded-lg">
                            <FaCalendarPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </div>
                        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                            {currentAppointment ? 'Editar Agendamento' : 'Novo Agendamento'}
                        </h2>
                    </div>
                    <button 
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-700/50 p-2 rounded-full transition"
                    >
                        <FaTimes />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* BODY: Formulário Clean */}
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-5">
                    
                    {/* Título */}
                    <div className="relative">
                        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-gray-500">
                            <FaTag className="text-xs" />
                        </div>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={title}
                            onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Título do compromisso (Ex: Reunião de Alinhamento)"
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 text-white text-sm rounded-xl pl-9 pr-4 py-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-primary focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent-primary placeholder-gray-600 transition-all"
                            required
                            autoFocus
                        />
                    </div>

                    {/* Datas (Grid Lado a Lado) */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                        <div>
                            <label className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1.5 flex items-center">
                                <FaClock className="mr-1.5" /> Início
                            </label>
                            <input
                                type="datetime-local"
                                value={startStr}
                                onChange={(e) => setStartStr(e.target.value)}
                                className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 text-white text-xs rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-primary"
                                required
                            />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1.5 flex items-center">
                                <FaClock className="mr-1.5" /> Fim
                            </label>
                            <input
                                type="datetime-local"
                                value={endStr}
                                onChange={(e) => setEndStr(e.target.value)}
                                className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 text-white text-xs rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-primary"
                                required
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Descrição */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1.5 flex items-center">
                            <FaAlignLeft className="mr-1.5" /> Detalhes (Opcional)
                        </label>
                        <textarea
                            value={description}
                            onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                            rows={3}
                            className="w-full bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 text-white text-sm rounded-xl px-4 py-3 focus:outline-none focus:border-accent-primary resize-none placeholder-gray-600 scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-gray-700"
                            placeholder="Adicione notas, pauta ou observações..."
                        />
                    </div>

                    {/* FOOTER: Botões de Ação */}
                    <div className="pt-2 flex justify-end gap-3">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={onClose}
                            className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-gray-800 border border-transparent hover:border-gray-600 transition"
                        >
                            Cancelar
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={isSaving}
                            className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-base-dark bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-500 shadow-lg shadow-violet-900/20 transition transform active:scale-95 flex items-center disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                        >
                            {isSaving ? (
                                'Salvando...'
                            ) : (
                                <>
                                    <FaSave className="mr-2" /> 
                                    {currentAppointment ? 'Salvar Alterações' : 'Agendar'}
                                </>
                            )}
                        </button>
                    </div>

                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/scheduler/components/SchedulerWidget.tsx =====

// src/modules/scheduler/components/SchedulerWidget.tsx

import React, { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Calendar, View, Views, SlotInfo } from 'react-big-calendar';
import { FaSync, FaCalendarAlt } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { localizer } from '../utils/calendarLocalizer';
import { useScheduler } from '../hooks/useScheduler';
import { AppointmentType } from '../types';
import { AppointmentModal } from './AppointmentModal'; // <--- Importação do Modal

import 'react-big-calendar/lib/css/react-big-calendar.css';
import '../styles/calendar.css'; 

interface SchedulerWidgetProps {
    mode?: 'FULL' | 'COMPACT' | 'MINI';
    height?: number | string;
    clientId?: string; 
}

export const SchedulerWidget: React.FC<SchedulerWidgetProps> = ({ 
    mode = 'FULL',
    height = '100%',
    clientId
}) => {
    
    // Agora desestruturamos também o createAppointment do hook
    const { events, isLoading, isSyncing, syncGoogle, createAppointment, updateAppointment } = useScheduler({ clientId });
    const [appointmentToEdit, setAppointmentToEdit] = useState<any | undefined>(undefined);

    // --- ESTADOS DE CONTROLE ---
    const [view, setView] = useState<View>(mode === 'FULL' ? Views.MONTH : Views.AGENDA);
    const [date, setDate] = useState(new Date());

    // --- ESTADOS DO MODAL (NOVO) ---
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [selectedSlot, setSelectedSlot] = useState<{ start: Date; end: Date } | undefined>(undefined);

    const availableViews: View[] = mode === 'FULL' 
        ? [Views.MONTH, Views.WEEK, Views.DAY, Views.AGENDA]
        : [Views.AGENDA];
    
    const showToolbar = mode !== 'MINI';

    // --- HANDLERS ---

    const handleNavigate = useCallback((newDate: Date) => {
        setDate(newDate);
    }, []);

    const handleViewChange = useCallback((newView: View) => {
        setView(newView);
    }, []);

    // ADICIONE ESTA FUNÇÃO:
    const handleDoubleClickEvent = useCallback((calendarEvent: any) => {
        // O 'resource' contém o objeto Appointment original que veio da API
        const originalAppointment = calendarEvent.resource;
        
        setAppointmentToEdit(originalAppointment); // Guarda os dados para o form
        setIsModalOpen(true); // Abre o mesmo modal de criação
    }, []);

    // AJUSTE NO HANDLE DO MODAL (Para limpar o estado ao fechar):
    const handleCloseModal = () => {
        setIsModalOpen(false);
        setAppointmentToEdit(undefined); // Limpa para não abrir "sujo" na próxima
        setSelectedSlot(undefined);
    };

    /**
     * Disparado quando o usuário clica ou arrasta em um espaço vazio da agenda.
     */
    const handleSelectSlot = ({ start, end }: { start: Date; end: Date }) => {
        setAppointmentToEdit(null); // <--- GARANTIA: Garante que não é edição
        setSelectedSlot({ start, end });
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    /**
     * Callback de sucesso do Modal.
     * Chama o hook para persistir no backend.
     */
    const handleSaveAppointment = async (data: { title: string; description: string; start: Date; end: Date }) => {
    try {
        if (appointmentToEdit) {
            // MODO EDIÇÃO
            await updateAppointment({
                id: appointmentToEdit.id,
                title: data.title,
                description: data.description,
                start: data.start,
                end: data.end
            });
        } else {
            // MODO CRIAÇÃO
            await createAppointment({
                title: data.title,
                description: data.description,
                start: data.start,
                end: data.end
            });
        }
        // O modal fecha sozinho após o sucesso (via props do modal) ou você fecha manualmente aqui se preferir
        handleCloseModal();
    } catch (error: any) {
        const msg = error.response?.data?.message || 'Erro ao salvar agendamento.';
        alert(msg);
        throw error;
    }
};

    const eventStyleGetter = (event: any) => {
        const isBlock = event.resource?.type === AppointmentType.BLOCK;
        const style = {
            backgroundColor: isBlock ? '#374151' : '#8B5CF6', 
            borderRadius: '4px',
            opacity: 0.9,
            color: 'white',
            border: '0px',
            display: 'block'
        };
        return { style };
    };

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-base-metallic rounded-xl border border-gray-700 shadow-lg overflow-hidden relative">
            
            {mode === 'FULL' && (
                <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-800">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <FaCalendarAlt className="text-accent-primary" />
                        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
                            {clientId ? 'Agenda do Cliente' : 'Agenda de Serviços'}
                        </h2>
                    </div>
                    
                    <button 
                        onClick={syncGoogle}
                        disabled={isSyncing}
                        className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded transition border border-gray-600"
                    >
                        <FaSync className={`w-3 h-3 ${isSyncing ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
                        <span>{isSyncing ? 'Sincronizando...' : 'Sincronizar Google'}</span>
                    </button>
                </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex-1 p-4 bg-gray-900" style={{ minHeight: mode === 'MINI' ? 200 : 500 }}>
                {isLoading ? (
                    <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500">
                        <FaSync className="animate-spin mr-2" /> Carregando agenda...
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <Calendar
                        localizer={localizer}
                        events={events}
                        startAccessor="start"
                        endAccessor="end"
                        style={{ height: '100%' }}
                        
                        view={view}
                        date={date}
                        onView={handleViewChange}
                        onNavigate={handleNavigate}
                        onDoubleClickEvent={handleDoubleClickEvent}
                        
                        // --- INTERATIVIDADE ---
                        selectable={true} // Habilita seleção
                        onSelectSlot={handleSelectSlot} // Handler do clique
                        scrollToTime={new Date(1970, 1, 1, 8)} // Rola para 08:00
                        longPressThreshold={10} // Melhor resposta no mobile
                        
                        views={availableViews}
                        toolbar={showToolbar}
                        eventPropGetter={eventStyleGetter}
                        messages={{
                            today: 'Hoje',
                            previous: 'Anterior',
                            next: 'Próximo',
                            month: 'Mês',
                            week: 'Semana',
                            day: 'Dia',
                            agenda: 'Lista',
                            date: 'Data',
                            time: 'Hora',
                            event: 'Evento',
                            noEventsInRange: 'Nenhum agendamento neste período.'
                        }}
                    />
                )}
            </div>

            {/* --- RENDERIZAÇÃO DO MODAL --- */}
            <AppointmentModal
                isOpen={isModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                initialSlot={selectedSlot}
                currentAppointment={appointmentToEdit}
                onConfirm={handleSaveAppointment}
            />
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/scheduler/hooks/useScheduler.ts =====

// src/modules/scheduler/hooks/useScheduler.ts

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { Appointment, CalendarEvent, AppointmentType } from '../types';

export interface UseSchedulerProps {
    clientId?: string;
    resourceId?: string;
}

// Interface para Criação
interface CreateAppointmentInput {
    title: string;
    description?: string;
    start: Date;
    end: Date;
}

// Interface para Edição (ID é obrigatório)
interface UpdateAppointmentInput {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    description?: string;
    start: Date;
    end: Date;
}

export function useScheduler({ clientId, resourceId }: UseSchedulerProps = {}) {
    
    const [events, setEvents] = useState<CalendarEvent[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
    const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing] = useState(false);

    // Converte o DTO da API para o formato do Calendário
    const mapToCalendarEvent = (apt: Appointment): CalendarEvent => ({
        id: apt.id,
        title: apt.type === AppointmentType.BLOCK ? 'Bloqueio Externo (Google)' : apt.title,
        start: new Date(apt.startTime),
        end: new Date(apt.endTime),
        resource: apt,
        isBlock: apt.type === AppointmentType.BLOCK
    });

    const fetchAppointments = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            setIsLoading(true);
            
            const params: Record<string, string> = {};
            if (clientId) params.clientId = clientId;
            if (resourceId) params.resourceId = resourceId;

            const response = await api.get('/scheduler/appointments', { params });
            const data = response.data as Appointment[];

            setEvents(data.map(mapToCalendarEvent));
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Erro ao buscar agenda:", error);
            setEvents([]);
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    }, [clientId, resourceId]);

    /**
     * Cria um novo agendamento.
     */
    const createAppointment = async (input: CreateAppointmentInput) => {
        try {
            await api.post('/scheduler/appointments', {
                title: input.title,
                description: input.description,
                startTime: input.start.toISOString(),
                endTime: input.end.toISOString(),
                clientId: clientId || undefined, 
                resourceId: resourceId || undefined
            });

            await fetchAppointments();
        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error("Erro ao criar agendamento:", error);
            throw error;
        }
    };

    /**
     * @method updateAppointment
     * @description Atualiza um agendamento existente via PATCH.
     */
    const updateAppointment = async (input: UpdateAppointmentInput) => {
        try {
            // Rota PATCH que criamos no Backend
            await api.patch(`/scheduler/appointments/${input.id}`, {
                title: input.title,
                description: input.description,
                startTime: input.start.toISOString(),
                endTime: input.end.toISOString(),
            });

            // Recarrega para refletir a mudança
            await fetchAppointments();

        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error("Erro ao atualizar agendamento:", error);
            throw error; // Lança para o componente tratar (ex: exibir erro de conflito)
        }
    };

    const syncGoogle = async () => {
        try {
            setIsSyncing(true);
            await api.post('/scheduler/sync');
            await fetchAppointments(); 
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Erro no sync:", error);
        } finally {
            setIsSyncing(false);
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        fetchAppointments();
    }, [fetchAppointments]);

    return {
        events,
        isLoading,
        isSyncing,
        syncGoogle,
        createAppointment,
        updateAppointment, // <--- Nova função exposta
        refresh: fetchAppointments
    };
}

// src/modules/scheduler/types.ts

export enum AppointmentType {
    MEETING = 'MEETING',
    SERVICE = 'SERVICE',
    BLOCK = 'BLOCK' // Bloqueio do Google
}

export enum AppointmentStatus {
    SCHEDULED = 'SCHEDULED',
    CONFIRMED = 'CONFIRMED',
    CANCELED = 'CANCELED',
    COMPLETED = 'COMPLETED'
}

export interface Appointment {
    id: string;
    resourceId: string; // ID do Técnico/User
    googleEventId: string | null;
    title: string;
    description: string | null;
    startTime: string; // ISO Date
    endTime: string;   // ISO Date
    type: AppointmentType;
    status: AppointmentStatus;
    // Futuro: color?: string;
}

// Interface para o Evento compatível com react-big-calendar
export interface CalendarEvent {
    id: string;
    title: string;
    start: Date;
    end: Date;
    resource: any; // Dados originais do Appointment
    isBlock: boolean; // Helper visual
}

// src/modules/settings/components/IntegrationCard.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { FaCheckCircle, FaExclamationCircle, FaArrowRight } from 'react-icons/fa';

interface IntegrationCardProps {
    title: string;
    description: string;
    icon: React.ElementType;
    isConnected: boolean;
    isLoading?: boolean;
    onConnect: () => void;
    onConfigure?: () => void; // Futuro: Para configurar opções da integração
    colorClass?: string; // Ex: text-blue-500
}

export const IntegrationCard: React.FC<IntegrationCardProps> = ({
    title,
    description,
    icon: Icon,
    isConnected,
    isLoading,
    onConnect,
    colorClass = "text-white"
}) => {
    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-800 border border-gray-700 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-lg flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-gray-600 transition-all group">
            
            <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
                    <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg bg-gray-900 shadow-inner ${colorClass}`}>
                        <Icon size={24} />
                    </div>
                    {isConnected ? (
                        <span className="flex items-center text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-green-400 bg-green-900/20 px-2 py-1 rounded-full border border-green-900/50">
                            <FaCheckCircle className="mr-1.5" /> Ativo
                        </span>
                    ) : (
                        <span className="flex items-center text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-500 bg-gray-900/50 px-2 py-1 rounded-full border border-gray-700">
                            <FaExclamationCircle className="mr-1.5" /> Desconectado
                        </span>
                    )}
                </div>

                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white mb-2">{title}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 leading-relaxed mb-6">
                    {description}
                </p>
            </div>

            <button
                onClick={onConnect}
                disabled={isLoading || isConnected}
                className={`w-full py-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${
                    isConnected 
                    ? 'bg-gray-700 text-gray-400 cursor-default'
                    : 'bg-accent-secondary hover:bg-violet-600 text-white shadow-md hover:shadow-lg active:scale-95'
                }`}
            >
                {isLoading ? (
                    <span className="animate-pulse">Processando...</span>
                ) : isConnected ? (
                    'Configurado'
                ) : (
                    <>Conectar Agora <FaArrowRight className="ml-2" /></>
                )}
            </button>
        </div>
    );
};


// ===== FILE: src/modules/settings/hooks/useIntegrations.ts =====

// src/modules/settings/hooks/useIntegrations.ts

import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';

// Interface que define o que o Backend retorna na rota /auth-url
interface GoogleAuthResponse {
    url: string;
}

export function useIntegrations() {
    
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    /**
     * Inicia o fluxo OAuth2 com o Google.
     * Busca a URL no backend e redireciona o navegador.
     */
    const connectGoogleCalendar = async () => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            // CORREÇÃO: Tipagem do retorno da API <GoogleAuthResponse>
            const response = await api.get<GoogleAuthResponse>('/scheduler/google/auth-url');
            
            if (response.data && response.data.url) {
                // Redireciona o usuário para a página de consentimento do Google
                window.location.href = response.data.url;
            } else {
                throw new Error("URL de autenticação não retornada pelo servidor.");
            }
        } catch (err: any) {
            console.error("Erro ao iniciar integração Google:", err);
            setError("Falha ao conectar com o Google. Tente novamente.");
            setIsLoading(false); 
        }
    };

    return {
        connectGoogleCalendar,
        isLoading,
        error
    };
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/settings/presentation/GoogleCallbackView.tsx =====

// src/modules/settings/presentation/GoogleCallbackView.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { api } from '@/core/services/api';
import { FaSync, FaCheckCircle, FaExclamationTriangle } from 'react-icons/fa';
// CORREÇÃO: Importar o AuthContext para saber o tenantId
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext'; 

export const GoogleCallbackView: React.FC = () => {
    
    const searchParams = useSearchParams();
    const { user } = useAuth(); // Pegamos o usuário logado
    
    const [status, setStatus] = useState<'LOADING' | 'SUCCESS' | 'ERROR'>('LOADING');
    const [message, setMessage] = useState('Processando autorização do Google...');
    
    const processedRef = useRef(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        const code = searchParams.get('code');
        const error = searchParams.get('error');

        // Evita processamento duplo (React Strict Mode)
        if (processedRef.current) return;
        
        if (error) {
            processedRef.current = true;
            setStatus('ERROR');
            setMessage('O acesso foi negado pelo usuário ou ocorreu um erro no Google.');
            return;
        }

        if (!code) {
            processedRef.current = true;
            setStatus('ERROR');
            setMessage('Código de autorização não encontrado na URL.');
            return;
        }

        // Se o user ainda não carregou, esperamos (o useEffect rodará de novo quando user mudar)
        // Isso evita redirecionar para 'undefined/dashboard'
        if (!user) return; 

        // Agora sim, marcamos como processado para não rodar 2x
        processedRef.current = true;

        const connectGoogle = async () => {
            try {
                await api.post('/scheduler/google/callback', { code });
                
                setStatus('SUCCESS');
                setMessage('Agenda conectada com sucesso! Redirecionando...');
                
                setTimeout(() => {
                    // CORREÇÃO: Redireciona para o contexto do Tenant correto
                    if (user.tenantId) {
                        window.location.href = `/${user.tenantId}/dashboard/settings`;
                    } else {
                        // Fallback de segurança
                        window.location.href = '/dashboard'; 
                    }
                }, 2000);

            } catch (err: any) {
                console.error("Erro no callback Google:", err);
                setStatus('ERROR');
                const msg = err.response?.data?.message || 'Falha ao trocar credenciais com o servidor.';
                setMessage(msg);
            }
        };

        connectGoogle();

    }, [searchParams, user]); // Adicionamos user nas dependências

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-base-dark flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 text-center">
            <div className="bg-base-metallic p-8 rounded-xl border border-gray-700 shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full">
                
                {status === 'LOADING' && (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center space-y-4">
                        <FaSync className="w-12 h-12 text-accent-primary animate-spin" />
                        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Conectando...</h2>
                        <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">{message}</p>
                    </div>
                )}

                {status === 'SUCCESS' && (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
                        <FaCheckCircle className="w-12 h-12 text-green-500" />
                        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Sucesso!</h2>
                        <p className="text-green-400 text-sm">{message}</p>
                    </div>
                )}

                {status === 'ERROR' && (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
                        <FaExclamationTriangle className="w-12 h-12 text-red-500" />
                        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Ops, algo deu errado.</h2>
                        <p className="text-red-400 text-sm">{message}</p>
                        <button 
                            onClick={() => window.location.href = user?.tenantId ? `/${user.tenantId}/dashboard` : '/login'}
                            className="mt-4 px-6 py-2 bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm transition"
                        >
                            Voltar ao Dashboard
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}

            </div>
        </div>
    );
}


// ===== FILE: src/modules/settings/presentation/IntegrationsTab.tsx =====

// src/modules/settings/presentation/IntegrationsTab.tsx

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { FaGoogle, FaWhatsapp } from 'react-icons/fa';

    // Hook do WhatsApp (Real)

    // Carrega status ao montar

        checkStatus();

    return (

                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Central de Integrações</h2>

                    Conecte suas ferramentas favoritas para automatizar agendamentos e comunicação.
                </p>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                
                {/* 1. Google Calendar */}
                <IntegrationCard 
                    title="Google Calendar"
                    description="Sincronize sua agenda pessoal para evitar conflitos. O sistema bloqueará horários ocupados automaticamente."
                    icon={FaGoogle}
                    colorClass="text-blue-500"
                    isConnected={false} // TODO: Implementar check de status do Google (v2)
                    isLoading={googleLoading}
                    onConnect={connectGoogleCalendar}
                />

                {/* 2. WhatsApp (Evolution) - Status Real */}
                <IntegrationCard 
                    title="WhatsApp Business"
                    description="Envie notificações automáticas e converse com clientes diretamente pelo CRM."
                    icon={FaWhatsapp}
                    colorClass="text-green-500"
                    isConnected={!!connectionStatus?.isConnected} // Estado REAL
                    isLoading={waLoading}
                    onConnect={() => {
                        // Redireciona para o CRM onde está o Widget completo com QR Code
                        window.location.href = window.location.pathname.replace('/settings', '/crm');
                    }}
                />

            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
